     我們是一家全球領先的現製飲品企業，聚焦為          We are a global leading freshly-made drinks company. We

     高質平價的現製果飲、茶飲、冰淇淋和咖啡等          consumers, including freshly-made fruit drinks, tea drinks,
     產品。我們旗下有現製茶飲品牌「蜜雪冰城」和         ice cream and coffee, typically priced around one U.S. dollar
     現磨咖啡品牌「幸運咖」。截至2024年12月31      (approximately RMB6) per item. We have two major brands
     日，我們通過加盟模式發展的門店網絡擁有超          – freshly-made tea drinks brand Mixue and freshly-made
     過46,000家門店，覆蓋中國及海外11個國家。      coffee brand Lucky Cup. Through a franchise model, we have
                                   cultivated a network of over 46,000 stores spanning China
                                   and 11 overseas countries as of December 31, 2024.

     及海外消費者提供現製果飲、茶飲及冰淇淋。          and ice cream to consumers both in China and overseas
     「蜜雪冰城」核心產品的價格通常為2元至8元人        through an extensive store network. Our core Mixue products
     民幣。「蜜雪冰城」的產品矩陣既包含常青款產         typically have a price range of RMB2 to RMB8. Our Mixue
     品，也包含季節性和區域性產品。               product offerings include both classic drinks and seasonal

     BUSINESS REVIEW AND OUTLOOK

     我們的加盟模式                       Our Franchise Model


     我們主要採用加盟模式來擴張我們的門店網           We primarily employ a franchise model to grow our store
     絡。在我們的加盟模式下，我們授權加盟商開          network. Under our franchise model, we authorize our
     設加盟門店並使用我們的品牌銷售現製飲品，          franchisees to sell freshly-made drinks through franchised
     同時向我們購買門店物料及設備用於其日常運          stores under our brands, while purchasing store supplies
     營。作為加盟門店的擁有者，加盟商對門店的          and equipment from us as part of their daily operations.
     經營業績負責。我們還規定加盟商須遵循我們          Franchisees are the owners of these stores and are
     全面、標準化的運營流程及要求。               accountable for their results of operations. We also stipulate
                                   that franchisees adhere to our comprehensive, standardized
                                   operational procedures and requirements.


     我們相信，與加盟商利益與共，是加盟商選擇          We believe our interest-aligned franchise model drives our
     我們的根本原因。我們通過一系列制度設計實          franchisees’ decision to partner with us. To align interests
     現與加盟商的利益與共。從收入模式而言，我          with our franchisees, we have systematically devised a range
     們不以加盟費和相關服務費為主要收入。2024        of policies and measures. In terms of our revenue model,
     年，我們2.5%的收入來自於加盟費和相關服務        franchise and related service fees are not our primary sources
     費。我們大規模的極致高效的供應鏈體系為加          of revenue. In 2024, 2.5% of our revenue was generated from
     盟商提供了具有競爭力的一站式解決方案，從          franchise and related services. Our expansive and highly
     而提高其利潤空間，提升消費者體驗。我們與          efficient supply chain provides franchisees with a competitive
     加盟商利益與共的合作，還體現在通過高度數          one-stop solution, improving their profitability and elevating
     字化的運營體系對加盟商進行標準化的管理以          consumer experience. We also demonstrate our interest-
     及持續不斷的支持與賦能，從而提升加盟商運          aligned cooperation with franchisees through standardized
     營效率。在利益與共的理念下，我們和加盟商          management and continuous support, empowering them
     共同實現了行業領先的門店規模，並建立了健          with a highly digitalized operational system to boost their
     康、可持續的加盟模式。                   efficiency. Under the philosophy of aligning interests with
                                   franchisees, we have together achieved an unparalleled store
                                   scale and built a healthy and sustainable franchise model.

     我們的供應鏈                        Our Supply Chain


     作為在中國現製飲品行業中最早設立中央工廠          As the first company to establish centralized factories in
     的企業，我們擁有大規模的高度數字化的端到          the freshly-made drinks industry in China, we operate an
     端供應鏈體系，核心飲品食材為100%自產。         expansive and highly digitalized end-to-end supply chain,
                                   with the core ingredients being 100% self-produced.

         主要包括食品類大宗商品、農產品及其他             stability of our supplies. Our raw materials procurement
         輔料等。直達產地的全球採購網絡以及龐             primarily comprises food commodities, agricultural
         大的採購規模，使我們能夠以低於行業平             products and other auxiliary materials. With our global
         均水平的價格採購眾多核心原材料，令我             procurement network extended to the origins of raw
         們以更高性價比的產品吸引消費者和加盟             materials, and our large procurement scale, we are able
         商。2024年12月，我們與中國領先的乳           to secure many core raw materials at prices below the
         製品公司君樂寶訂立戰略合作關係，合資             industry average. This enables us to attract consumers
         建設「雪王牧場」。通過此次合作，預期             and franchisees by offering products with greater

         奶，從而提高我們的供應鏈能力及產品質             strategic collaboration with Junlebao, a leading dairy

幣計值，而全球發售所得款項淨額以港元計值。           and expenditures were denominated in Renminbi, while

     公眾持股量充足性                       SUFFICIENCY OF PUBLIC FLOAT

     上市規則第8.08條規定，尋求上市的證券必須         Rule 8.08 of the Listing Rules requires there to be an open
     有一個公開市場，且發行人的上市證券須維持           market in the securities for which listing is sought and a
     足夠的公眾持股量。這一般指無論何時，發行           sufficient public float of an issuer’s listed securities to be

     潛在分拆的狀況                                      Status of the Potential Spin-off


     如招股章程所披露，考慮到本公司海外業務的                         As disclosed in the Prospectus, having considered, among
     規模及快速增長，以及其與本公司在中國內地                         others, the size and rapid growth of the Company’s overseas
     的業務在地理位置及市場側重方面的明確區分                         businesses and its clear delineation with the Company’s
     等因素，本公司擬保留於上市後三年內分拆海                         business in mainland China in terms of geographical
     外業務的可能性（「潛在分拆」）。因此，本公司                       locations and market focus, the Company wishes to retain
     已申請豁免嚴格遵守第15項應用指引第3(b)段                      the possibility to spin off its overseas businesses within three
     項下有關本集團海外業務潛在分拆的三年限制                         years after the Listing (the “Potential Spin-off”). As such,
     規定，且聯交所已授出有關豁免。潛在分拆仍                         the Company has applied for, and the Stock Exchange has
     將受第15項應用指引的其他規定所規限。                          granted, a waiver from strict compliance with the three-
                                                  year restriction requirement under paragraph 3(b) of Practice
                                                  Note 15 in relation to the Potential Spin-off of the overseas
                                                  businesses of the Group. The Potential Spin-off will remain
                                                  subject to other requirements of Practice Note 15.

事會例會，並將事項列入董事會例會議程。         days are given for all regular Board meetings to provide all
                            Directors with an opportunity to attend and include matters in
                            the agenda for regular Board meetings.

略目標的基本要素。               level as an essential element in maintaining the Company’s
                        sustainable development and achieving its strategic goals.

     風險管理及內部控制                 RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROLS

     董事會確認其對風險管理及內部控制系統之責      The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk
     任，並負責檢討該等系統之有效性。該等系統      management and internal control systems and for reviewing
     旨在管理而非消除未能達成業務目標之風險，      their effectiveness. Such systems are designed to manage
     且僅可就重大失實陳述或損失提供合理而非絕      rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business
     對之保證。                     objectives, and can only provide reasonable and not absolute
                               assurance against material misstatement or loss.


     董事會全權負責評估及釐定本公司為實現戰略      The Board has the overall responsibility for evaluating and
     目標而願意承擔之風險性質及程度，並建立及      determining the nature and extent of the risks it is willing to
     維持適當且有效之風險管理及內部控制機制。      take in achieving the Company’s strategic objectives, and
                               establishing and maintaining appropriate and effective risk
                               management and internal control mechanisms.


     審計委員會協助董事會監督風險管理及內部控      The Audit Committee assists the Board in leading the
     制系統之設計、實施及監察。             management and overseeing their design, implementation
                               and monitoring of the risk management and internal control
                               systems.

本公司制定了信息披露管理制度，通過標準化      The Company has developed an Information Disclosure
程序確保所有重大未公開信息均通過指定渠道      Management System to ensure that all material undisclosed
向市場及時披露。根據該制度，本公司須在知      information is disclosed to the market through designated
道任何內幕消息後、或有可能造成虛假市場的      channels in a timely manner through standardized procedures.
情況下，在合理地切實可行的範圍內，及時向      Under the system, the Company is required to disclose to
公眾披露。自上市日期起至最後實際可行日       the public any inside information as soon as reasonably
期，本公司嚴格按照上市規則等法律法規的要      practicable after it becomes aware of it or is likely to create
求進行信息披露，並無作出任何虛假陳述、誤      a false market. From the Listing Date and up to the Latest
導性陳述或重大遺漏，以確保投資者能夠公       Practicable Date, the Company has disclosed information
平、及時、有效地接收所披露的信息。         in strict compliance with the requirements of the laws and
                          regulations including the Listing Rules without any false
                          statements, misleading statements or material omissions,
                          to ensure investors will be able to receive the disclosed
                          information fairly, timely and effectively.


本集團各業務部門及職能部門已建立常態化內      All divisions/departments conducted internal control
部監控評估機制，定期審查可能影響本集團業      assessment regularly to identify risks that potentially impact
務之潛在風險，涵蓋關鍵營運及財務流程、監      the business of the Group and various aspects, including key
管合規與信息安全等核心領域。各部門每年執      operational and financial processes, regulatory compliance

策。                        annually to confirm that control policies are properly complied
                          with by each division/department.


管理層已就截至2024年12月31日止年度之風   The management has reported to the Audit Committee on
險管理及內部監控制度有效性，向審計委員會      the effectiveness of the risk management and internal control

     意見                         Opinion


     我們已審計第99至231頁所載蜜雪冰城股份有     We have audited the consolidated financial statements of
     限公司（「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（「貴集團」）的   MIXUE Group (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (the
     綜合財務報表，其中包括於2024年12月31日    “Group”) set out on pages 99 to 231, which comprise
     的綜合財務狀況表與截至該日止年度的綜合損       the consolidated statement of financial position as at 31
     益表、綜合全面收益表、綜合權益變動表及綜       December 2024, and the consolidated statement of profit or
     合現金流量表，以及綜合財務報表附註，包括       loss, the consolidated statement of comprehensive income,
     重大會計政策資料。                  the consolidated statement of changes in equity and the
                                consolidated statement of cash flows for the year then ended,
                                and notes to the consolidated financial statements, including
                                material accounting policy information.


     我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據國際會計       In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a
     準則理事會（「國際會計準則理事會」）頒佈的國     true and fair view of the consolidated financial position of
     際財務報告會計準則真實而中肯地反映貴集團       the Group as at 31 December 2024, and of its consolidated
     於2024年12月31日的綜合財務狀況及截至該    financial performance and its consolidated cash flows for
     日止年度的綜合財務表現及綜合現金流量，並       the year then ended in accordance with IFRS Accounting
     已遵照香港公司條例的披露規定妥為擬備。        Standards as issued by the International Accounting
                                Standards Board (“IASB”) and have been properly prepared
                                in compliance with the disclosure requirements of the Hong
                                Kong Companies Ordinance.

               準則理事會批准的國際會計準則及                Accounting Standards Board (the “IASB”), and
               常務詮釋委員會詮釋以及香港公司                International Accounting Standards and Standing
               條例的披露規定。                       Interpretations Committee interpretations
                                              approved by the International Accounting
                                              Standards Board and the disclosure requirements
                                              of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.


               本集團已自2021年1月1日（國際財             All IFRS Accounting Standards effective for the
               務報告會計準則過渡日期）起提早採               accounting period commencing from 1 January
               納於2024年1月1日開始的會計期間             2024, together with the relevant transitional
               生效的所有國際財務報告會計準則                provisions, have been early adopted by the Group
               連同相關過渡性條文。                     from 1 January 2021 (the date of transition to IFRS
                                              Accounting Standards).


               綜合財務報表根據歷史成本慣例編                The consolidated financial statements have been
               製，惟以公允價值計量且其變動計                prepared under the historical cost convention,
               入損益的金融資產及指定以公允價                except for financial assets at fair value through
               值計量且其變動計入其他全面收益                profit or loss and equity investments designated
               的股權投資以公允價值計量除外。                at fair value through other comprehensive income
               該等財務報表以人民幣（「人民幣」）              which have been measured at fair value. These

     2.1 擬備基準（續）                    2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION (Continued)


       綜合基準                             Basis of consolidation

       綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬                  The consolidated financial statements include
       公司（統稱「本集團」）截至2024 年              the financial statements of the Company and
       12月31日止年度的財務報表。附屬                its subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the
       公司為本公司直接或間接控制的實                  “Group”) for the year ended 31 December 2024.
       體（包括結構性實體）。當本集團因                 A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured
       參與投資對象的業務而就可變回報                  entity), directly or indirectly, controlled by the
       承擔風險或有權獲得可變回報，並                  Company. Control is achieved when the Group is
       有能力透過對投資對象的權力（即賦                 exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its
       予本集團目前主導投資對象相關業                  involvement with the investee and has the ability
       務的能力的現有權利）影響該等回報                 to affect those returns through its power over the
       時，則視為擁有控制權。                      investee (i.e., existing rights that give the Group
                                        the current ability to direct the relevant activities of
                                        the investee).


       一般假定大多數投票權導致控制                   Generally, there is a presumption that a majority
       權。倘本公司於投資對象擁有的投                  of voting rights results in control. When the
       票權或類似權利不足大多數，則本                  Company has less than a majority of the voting or
       集團於評估其是否擁有對投資對象                  similar rights of an investee, the Group considers
       的權力時會考慮一切相關事實及情                  all relevant facts and circumstances in assessing
       況，包括：                            whether it has power over an investee, including:


       (a)   與投資對象其他具投票權的持              (a)   the contractual arrangement with the other
             有人的合約安排；                         vote holders of the investee;


       (b)   其他合約安排所產生的權利；              (b)   rights arising from other contractual
             及                                arrangements; and


       (c)   本集團的投票權及潛在投票               (c)   the Group’s voting rights and potential voting
             權。                               rights.

               附屬公司的財務報表與本公司於相                The financial statements of the subsidiaries are
               同報告期間內採納貫徹一致的會計                prepared for the same reporting period as the
               政策編製。附屬公司的業績自本集                Company, using consistent accounting policies.
               團取得控制權當日起綜合入賬，並                The results of subsidiaries are consolidated from
               繼續綜合入賬直至該項控制權終止                the date on which the Group obtains control, and
               當日為止。                          continue to be consolidated until the date that
                                              such control ceases.


               即使會導致非控股權益出現虧損結                Profit or loss and each component of other
               餘，損益及其他全面收入的各個組                comprehensive income (“OCI”) are attributed to
               成部分仍會歸屬於本集團母公司擁                the owners of the parent of the Group and to the
               有人及非控股權益。所有與本集團                non-controlling interests, even if this results in the
               成員公司間交易有關的集團內公司                non-controlling interests having a deficit balance.
               間資產及負債、權益、收入、開支                All intra-group assets and liabilities, equity,
               及現金流量於綜合入賬時全數抵銷。               income, expenses and cash flows relating to
                                              transactions between members of the Group are
                                              eliminated in full on consolidation.


               倘事實及情況顯示以上所述三項控                The Group reassesses whether or not it controls
               制權元素中的一項或多項有變動，                an investee if facts and circumstances indicate
               則本集團會重新評估其是否擁有對                that there are changes to one or more of the three
               投資對象的控制權。於附屬公司擁                elements of control described above. A change
               有權權益的變動（並無失去控制權）               in the ownership interest of a subsidiary, without
               於入賬時列作權益交易。                    a loss of control, is accounted for as an equity
                                              transaction.

               於聯營公司投資                       Investment in an associate

               聯營公司是指本集團長期持有其權                An associate is an entity in which the Group has a
               益股票權一般不少於20%且對其擁               long term interest of generally not less than 20%
               有重大影響力的實體。重大影響力                of the equity voting rights and over which it has
               是指有權參與被投資公司的財務及                significant influence. Significant influence is the
               經營政策的決策，但並非控制或共                power to participate in the financial and operating
               同控制該等政策。                       policy decisions of the investee, but is not control
                                              or joint control over those policies.


               本集團於聯營公司投資乃根據會計                The Group’s investment in an associate is stated
               權益法，按本集團應佔的資產淨值                in the consolidated statement of financial position
               扣除任何減值虧損於綜合財務狀況                at the Group’s share of net assets under the equity
               表內呈列。任何可能存在的不同會                method of accounting, less any impairment losses.
               計準則已作出調整以使其一致。                 Adjustments are made to bring into line any
                                              dissimilar accounting policies that may exist.


               本集團應佔聯營公司收購後業績及                The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results
               其他全面收入份額分別計入綜合損                and other comprehensive income of an associate
               益表及綜合其他全面收入。此外，                is included in the consolidated statement of profit
               倘於聯營公司的權益內直接確認一                or loss and consolidated other comprehensive
               項變動，則本集團會於綜合權益變                income, respectively. In addition, when there has
               動表內確認其於任何有關變動的應                been a change recognised directly in the equity of
               佔份額（倘適用）。本集團與聯營公               the associate, the Group recognises its share of
               司交易所產生的未變現盈虧，均按                any changes, when applicable, in the consolidated
               本集團於聯營公司的投資為限進行                statement of changes in equity. Unrealised gains
               抵銷，除非未變現虧損證明已轉讓                and losses resulting from transactions between
               資產出現減值則除外。收購聯營公                the Group and its associate are eliminated to the
               司產生的商譽計入本集團於聯營公                extent of the Group’s investment in the associate,
               司的投資的一部分。                      except where unrealised losses provide evidence
                                              of an impairment of the assets transferred.
                                              Goodwill arising from the acquisition of associate
                                              is included as part of the Group’s investment in an
                                              associate.

       公允價值計量                         Fair value measurement

       本集團於各報告期末按公允價值計                The Group measures its certain of wealthy
       量其若干理財產品及股權投資。公                management products and equity investments at
       允價值為於計量日期在市場參與者                fair value at the end of each reporting period. Fair
       間進行的有序交易中出售資產所收                value is the price that would be received to sell
       取或轉移負債所支付的價格。公允                an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly
       價值計量乃假設出售資產或轉移負                transaction between market participants at the
       債的交易於資產或負債的主要市場                measurement date. The fair value measurement
       進行，或在未有主要市場的情況                 is based on the presumption that the transaction
       下，則假設於資產或負債的最有利                to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place
       市場進行。主要或最有利市場必須                either in the principal market for the asset or
       為本集團可進入的市場。資產或負                liability, or in the absence of a principal market,
       債的公允價值乃基於市場參與者為                in the most advantageous market for the asset or
       資產或負債定價時所用的假設計量                liability. The principal or the most advantageous
       （假設市場參與者以符合彼等最佳經               market must be accessible by the Group. The fair
       濟利益的方式行事）。                     value of an asset or a liability is measured using
                                      the assumptions that market participants would
                                      use when pricing the asset or liability, assuming
                                      that market participants act in their economic best
                                      interest.


       非金融資產的公允價值計量會計及                A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset
       一名市場參與者透過以最大限度使                takes into account a market participant’s ability to
       用該資產達致最佳用途，或透過將                generate economic benefits by using the asset in
       資產出售予將以最大限度使用該資                its highest and best use or by selling it to another
       產達致最佳用途的另一名市場參與                market participant that would use the asset in its
       者而產生經濟利益的能力。                   highest and best use.


       本集團使用適用於不同情況且具備                The Group uses valuation techniques that are
       足夠可用數據以計量公允價值的估                appropriate in the circumstances and for which
       值方法，以盡量使用相關可觀察輸                sufficient data are available to measure fair value,
       入數據及盡量減少使用不可觀察輸                maximising the use of relevant observable inputs
       入數據。                           and minimising the use of unobservable inputs.

       下，就該項資產所屬的現金產生單                other assets or groups of assets, in which case

       產生期間自損益表扣除。在符合確                      as repairs and maintenance, is normally charged

       將予以資本化並重置資產的賬面價                      in which it is incurred. In situations where the
       值。倘物業、廠房及設備的主要部                      recognition criteria are satisfied, the expenditure
       分須定期替換，則本集團將該等部                      for a major inspection is capitalised in the carrying
       分確認為具特定可使用年期的個別                      amount of the asset as a replacement. Where

               金融資產減值                                 Impairment of financial assets

               本集團就所有並非以公允價值計量                        The Group recognises an allowance for expected
               且其變動計入損益持有的債務工具                        credit losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not
               確 認 預 期 信 用 損 失（「 預 期 信 用 損            held at fair value through profit or loss. ECLs are
               失」）撥備。預期信用損失乃以根據                       based on the difference between the contractual
               合約應付的合約現金流量與本集團                        cash flows due in accordance with the contract
               預期收取的所有現金流量之間的差                        and all the cash flows that the Group expects to
               額為基準，按原實際利率的近似值                        receive, discounted at an approximation of the
               貼現。預期現金流量將包括來自銷                        original effective interest rate. The expected cash
               售所持有抵押品或其他信用增級的                        flows will include cash flows from the sale of
               現金流量，此乃合約條款不可或缺                        collateral held or other credit enhancements that
               的部分。                                   are integral to the contractual terms.


               一般方法                                   General approach


               預期信用損失於兩個階段進行確                         ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit
               認。對於自初步確認後並無顯著增                        exposures for which there has not been a
               加的信貸風險，預期信用損失就可                        significant increase in credit risk since initial
               能於未來12 個月內（12 個月預期信                    recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses
               用損失）出現的違約事件所導致的信                       that result from default events that are possible
               用損失計提撥備。對於自初步確認                        within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For
               後有顯著增加的信貸風險，須在信                        those credit exposures for which there has been
               用損失風險預期的剩餘年期計提虧                        a significant increase in credit risk since initial
               損撥備，不論違約事件於何時發生                        recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit
              （全期預期信用損失）。                             losses expected over the remaining life of the
                                                      exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a
                                                      lifetime ECL).

       股份支付                           Share-based payments

       本公司設有一項股份獎勵安排，為                The Company operates a share award

       方式收取酬金，而員工則提供服務                operations. Employees (including directors) of
       以換取股權工具（
              「股權結算交易」
                     ）。               the Group receive remuneration in the form of
                                      share-based payments, whereby employees

       與員工進行的以股權結算的交易的                The cost of equity-settled transactions with
       成本乃參考授出日期的公允價值計                employees is measured by reference to the fair
       量。                             value at the date at which they are granted.

11. 所得稅                               11. INCOME TAX

   本集團須按實體基準就於本集團成員公司                    The Group is subject to income tax on an entity basis on
   註冊及經營所在稅務司法管轄區產生或取                    profits arising in or derived from the tax jurisdictions in
   得的利潤繳納所得稅。                            which members of the Group are domiciled and operate.


   中國企業所得稅                               PRC corporate income tax


   根 據《 中 華 人 民 共 和 國 企 業 所 得 稅 法 》      Under the Law of the PRC on Enterprise Income Tax
  （「《企業所得稅法》」）及《企業所得稅法實                  (the “EIT Law”) and Implementation Regulation of the
   施條例》，本集團中國附屬公司的企業所                    EIT Law, the EIT rate of the Group’s PRC subsidiaries is
   得稅稅率為25%，惟獲得下文所載稅項豁                   25% unless subject to tax exemption set out below.
   免者除外。


   從事「西部地區鼓勵類產業」業務的若干                    Certain subsidiaries that are engaged in businesses in
   附屬公司可享有15%的企業所得稅優惠稅                   the “Encouraged Industries in the Western Region” and
   率。                                    eligible for the preferential EIT rate of 15%.


   於海南自由貿易港註冊及運營，且符合核                    Certain subsidiaries that are domiciled and operate in
   心業務收入佔60%以上標準的若干附屬公                   Hainan Free Trade Port which meet the criteria of having
   司，可享有15%的企業所得稅優惠稅率。                   more than 60% of the revenue generated from their core
                                         businesses are eligible for the preferential EIT rate of
                                         15%.


   若干附屬公司從事農產品預處理，可免徵                    Certain subsidiaries are engaged in agricultural product
   有關稅項。                                 pre-treatment and eligible for relevant tax exemptions.


   香港利得稅                                 Hong Kong profit tax


   截至2024年及2023年12月31日止年度，               Hong Kong profits tax has been provided at the rate of
   香港利得稅已按於香港產生的估計應課稅                    16.5% on the estimated assessable profits arising in
   利潤的16.5%計提撥備，惟本集團一間附                  Hong Kong for the years ended 31 December 2024 and
   屬公司根據利得稅兩級制屬合資格實體除                    2023, except for one subsidiary of the Group which is
   外。該附屬公司的首2,000,000港元應課                a qualifying entity under the two-tiered profits tax rates
   稅利潤按8.25%的稅率徵稅，而餘下應課                  regime. The first HK$2,000,000 of assessable profits of
   稅利潤則按16.5%的稅率徵稅。                      this subsidiary are taxed at 8.25% and the remaining
                                         assessable profits are taxed at 16.5%.

                              FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS

  管理層已評估現金及現金等價物、定期存         Management has assessed that the fair values of cash
  款及受限制現金的即期部分、貿易應收款         and cash equivalents, current portion of time deposits
  項、計入預付款項、其他應收款項及其他         and restricted cash, trade receivables, financial assets
  資產的金融資產、計息銀行及其他借款、         included in prepayments, other receivables and other
  貿易應付款項、計入其他應付款項及應計         assets, interest-bearing bank and other borrowings,
  費用的金融負債的公允價值均與賬面值相         trade payables, financial liabilities included in other
  若，主要是由於該等工具於短期內到期。         payables and accruals approximate to their carrying
  管理層亦已評估定期存款及受限制現金的         amounts largely due to the short-term maturities of
  非即期部分的公允價值與賬面值相若。          these instruments. Management has also assessed the
                             fair value of non-current portion of time deposits and
                             restricted cash approximate to its carrying amount.


  本集團財務中心負責釐定金融工具公允價         The Group’s finance centre is responsible for
  值計量的政策及程序。財務中心直接向首         determining the policies and procedures for the fair
  席財務官及董事會報告。於各報告日期，         value management of financial instruments. The finance
  財務中心分析金融工具的價值變動並釐定         centre reports directly to the chief financial officer and
  估值中適用的主要輸入值。估值由首席財         the board of directors. At each reporting date, the
  務官審核及批准。董事會就估值過程及結         finance centre analyses the movements in the values of
  果進行討論，以作年度財務報告。            financial instruments and determines the major inputs
                             applied in the valuation. The valuation is reviewed and
                             approved by the chief financial officer. The valuation
                             process and results are discussed with the board of
                             directors for annual financial reporting.


  金融資產及負債的公允價值按該工具於自         The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are
  願雙方當前交易（非被迫或清算性出售）         included at the amount at which the instrument could
  中可交換的金額列賬。估計公允價值時採         be exchanged in a current transaction between willing
  用以下方法及假設。                  parties, other than in a forced or liquidation sale. The
                             following methods and assumptions were used to
                             estimate the fair values.


  就按公允價值計量且其變動計入其他全面         For the fair value of the unlisted equity investments
  收益的非上市股權投資的公允價值而言，         at fair value through other comprehensive income,
  管理層採用具有一些不可觀察輸入值的貼         management adopts discounted cash flow valuation
  現現金流估值模型。                  model with some unobservable inputs.

          本集團主要的金融工具包括以公允價值計            The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise

          團擁有直接源自其運營的多種其他金融資            other borrowings. The Group has various other financial
          產和負債，例如貿易應收款項及貿易應付            assets and liabilities such as trade receivables and trade
          款項。                           payables, which arise directly from its operations.


          本集團已採納庫務政策，當中載明購買及            The Group has adopted a treasury policy which sets out
          贖回理財產品的整體原則及詳細審批流             overall principles as well as detailed approval processes
          程。                            of the purchase and redemption of wealth management
                                        products.

          險、利率風險、信貸風險及流動性風險。            instruments are foreign currency risk, interest rate risk,
          本公司管理層會覆核及協議控制各類風險            credit risk and liquidity risk. The management of the

  整資本結構，本集團或會調整派付予權益                    conditions and the risk characteristics of the underlying

  發行新股。本集團不受任何外部施加的資                    the Group may adjust the dividend payment to equity

  序並無任何變動。                              shares. The Group is not subject to any externally
